Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the lie_groups object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 522. The moduli space has dimension at most 13 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to lie_groups theory, we decompose the problem into 522 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 13. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
